## Classification

Green: nontrivial body without a recognized unsupported marker. Amber: guarded/unsupported or conditionally defined. Blue: simple no-op/constant-return body, which may be intentional. Grey: inherited stub, explicit placeholder, or unsupported-only body.

The shader selector denominator contains names and prefix families declared by dispatch code. Families may overlap; it is not a complete ISA reference. Definitions in the other panels count known source definitions, not missing features. Compiler-defaulted C++ functions are identified in the evidence. Macro expansions, third-party code and test fixtures are excluded.

See [tracker documentation](../../tools/progress_tracker/README.md) for scope and limitations.
